2026-11-25T22:19:29.4568826Z SOURCE_SHA="$GITHUB_SHA"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4568973Z SHORT_SHA="${SOURCE_SHA::7}"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4569119Z PUBLISH_MODE="none"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4569255Z PR_IMAGE_TAG=""
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4569378Z PUSH_OUTPUT="false"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4569511Z 
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4569678Z # workflow_call path (called from integration.yml). The planner only
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4569940Z # invokes us when a build is actually needed (no pin/cache match), so
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4570189Z # we always publish. The publish mode follows the caller's event:
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4570446Z # pull_request -> pr-scoped image (consumed by the calling Integration
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4570700Z # run via digest output, not by tag polling); main/schedule/dispatch
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4570975Z # -> pin-refresh image (operator opens a reviewed image-pin.json PR
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4571177Z # afterwards).
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4571323Z if [[ -n "$EXPECTED_FINGERPRINT" ]]; then
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4571484Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4571697Z   if [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "pull_request" && "$PR_HEAD_REPO" == "$GITHUB_REPOSITORY" ]]; then
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4572036Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pr"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4572215Z     PR_IMAGE_TAG="${IMAGE_NAME}:pr-${PR_NUMBER}-${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4572448Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4572789Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4572995Z   elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "schedule" || "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4573211Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4573338Z   else
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4573503Z     # Untrusted PR (fork) reaching workflow_call should have been
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4573725Z     # blocked at the planner; defend in depth here too.
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4574026Z     echo "::error::workflow_call invoked from unsupported event ($EVENT_NAME, head_repo=$PR_HEAD_REPO)." >&2
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4574282Z     exit 1
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4574398Z   fi
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4574569Z # Standalone path: push to main, weekly schedule, or manual dispatch.
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4574828Z # These refresh the reviewed image-pin.json digest. The pull_request
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4575080Z # trigger no longer exists on this workflow; PR builds reach us only
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4575293Z # through the workflow_call branch above.
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4575507Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "push" && "$REF" == "refs/heads/main" ]]; then
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4575702Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4575837Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4575984Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "schedule" ]]; then
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4576144Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="true"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4576273Z   PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4576431Z elif [[ "$EVENT_NAME" == "workflow_dispatch" ]]; then
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4576612Z   PUSH_OUTPUT="${DISPATCH_PUSH}"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4576778Z   if [[ "$DISPATCH_PUSH" == "true" ]]; then
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4576936Z     PUBLISH_MODE="pin"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4577063Z   fi
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4577166Z fi
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4577268Z {
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4577386Z   echo "source_sha=${SOURCE_SHA}"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4577541Z   echo "short_sha=${SHORT_SHA}"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4577710Z   echo "cache_key=ijt-browser-ci-${SOURCE_SHA}"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4577884Z   echo "push=${PUSH_OUTPUT}"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4578159Z   echo "publish_mode=${PUBLISH_MODE}"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4578328Z   echo "pr_image_tag=${PR_IMAGE_TAG}"
2026-11-25T22:19:29.4578484Z } >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
